ANNUAL STRATEGIC REVIEW 2026 — FULL NARRATIVE
======================================================================


SECTION 1. Strategic Context and Operating Notes
Stakeholders across the organization continue to rally around the Atlas migration despite the inevitable headwinds. Cross-functional alignment on Northwind compliance improved markedly after the mid-year operating review. Finance modeled several scenarios for Project Helios, stress-testing the assumptions behind the plan.
Progress on Northwind compliance was uneven across regions, but the underlying trajectory remains encouraging. Risks associated with Project Helios were re-scored and folded into the enterprise risk register. The narrative around the Atlas migration matured from aspiration to disciplined, milestone-driven execution.
Investment in Project Helios reflects a deliberate, patient view of where durable value is created. Customer-facing teams reported that the Atlas migration is beginning to show up in retention conversations. Talent and hiring decisions were increasingly made in service of Northwind compliance rather than around it.
Cross-functional alignment on the Atlas migration improved markedly after the mid-year operating review. Finance modeled several scenarios for Northwind compliance, stress-testing the assumptions behind the plan. The leadership team reaffirmed its commitment to Project Helios as a defining initiative for the year.
Risks associated with Northwind compliance were re-scored and folded into the enterprise risk register. The narrative around Project Helios matured from aspiration to disciplined, milestone-driven execution. Stakeholders across the organization continue to rally around the Atlas migration despite the inevitable headwinds.
Customer-facing teams reported that Project Helios is beginning to show up in retention conversations. Talent and hiring decisions were increasingly made in service of the Atlas migration rather than around it. Progress on Northwind compliance was uneven across regions, but the underlying trajectory remains encouraging.
Finance modeled several scenarios for the Atlas migration, stress-testing the assumptions behind the plan. The leadership team reaffirmed its commitment to Northwind compliance as a defining initiative for the year. Investment in Project Helios reflects a deliberate, patient view of where durable value is created.

Risks associated with Northwind compliance were re-scored and folded into the enterprise risk register.
The leadership team reaffirmed its commitment to the Atlas migration as a defining initiative for the year. Investment in Northwind compliance reflects a deliberate, patient view of where durable value is created. Customer-facing teams reported that Project Helios is beginning to show up in retention conversations.
